## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 gpu-6000pro-2 的 cuda:0 和 cuda:1（项目配置白名单）
- Registry/cron server 名必须用 `gpu-6000pro-2`（不是 `westb-52439`，后者不在系统列表 → cron SSH 失败 → 误杀实验）
- OLMo 必须用 `allenai/OLMo-1B-hf`（非 OLMo-1B），LoRA targets 是三个模块 `q_proj,k_proj,v_proj`
- GPT-2 XL LoRA targets 是 `c_attn,c_proj`（Conv1D 不是 nn.Linear）
- Pipeline 脚本提交前必须 `bash -n script.sh` 验证语法（olmo_001 事故教训）
- submit_training_job 的命令必须自带环境激活（如 `source /root/miniconda3/bin/activate && ...`）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：gpu-6000pro-2，2× NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 98GB
- 工作目录：`/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est`
- Python：conda base，`/root/miniconda3/bin/python`（aml-train 镜像）
- 模型路径：
  - Pythia-1.4B: `/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b/`
  - OLMo-1B-hf: `/root/autodl-tmp/models/olmo-1b-hf/`
  - GPT-2 XL: `/root/autodl-tmp/models/gpt2-xl/`
  - DeBERTa-v3-large: `/root/autodl-tmp/models/deberta-v3-large/`
- 数据：`/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/data/`（depth_0.jsonl = 5000 samples）
- Checkpoints: `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/checkpoints/{olmo,gpt2xl,pythia_temp09}/`
- 日志：`/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/logs/`
- 活跃进程：无（两块 GPU 均空闲）
- Cron：reconcile 返回 0 条活跃

### 3. 📊 当前状态
刚收到 cross_model_olmo_002 失败回调（process dead 5×, no W&B metrics），已派 Worker 诊断（worker_diagnose_cross_model_olmo_002_20260514T182106）。

同时 cross_model_gpt2xl_001 进入 ANALYZING（pipeline 完成），已派 Worker 分析结果（worker_analyze_cross_model_gpt2xl_001_20260514T182129）。

**两个 Worker 都还没回调**——下一个 session 需要等回调或主动 get_worker_status 检查。

Registry 关键状态：
| exp_id | status | conclusion |
|--------|--------|------------|
| cross_model_olmo_002 | DONE | failed（第 2 次，olmo_001 脚本语法错 → 修复后重跑仍失败）|
| cross_model_gpt2xl_001 | ANALYZING | 待分析 |
| pythia_topk50_001 | PREPARING | 等 GPU 空出 |
| pythia_temp09_001 | DONE | success（4-class 76.25%）|
| mvp_pipeline_pythia_002 | DONE | success（4-class 78.2%）|

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- Worker diagnose olmo_002 回调 → 读诊断 JSON，按 error_type 决策：infrastructure 可自动修→重提交(第3次)；resource/其他→汇报 Director
- Worker analyze gpt2xl_001 回调 → 读分类指标，update_registry(metrics/conclusion)，与 Pythia baseline 对比，汇报 Director
- gpt2xl_001 分析完成 + GPU 空出 → 立即提交 pythia_topk50_001（cuda:0 或 cuda:1）
- OLMo 修复后 → 重提交 cross_model_olmo_003（cuda:0）
- 3×3 矩阵 nucleus 列完成后 → 每个模型接排 temp09 → topk50
- **两个 Worker 回调后必须汇报 Director**（带 [资源] 行）

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- D001: MVP PASS（Pythia 4-class 78.2%），进入 Research phase
- D002: Dream 三档审稿完成，Atlas/EDD 为 primary contribution，DCOL 为 secondary
- 当前目标：填满 3×3 矩阵（3 模型 × 3 解码策略），2/9 done, 1/9 analyzing, 1/9 preparing, 1/9 failed×2, 4/9 待排
- OLMo 连续失败 2 次是关键瓶颈——如果第 3 次仍失败，需要考虑换模型（如 GPT-Neo / Llama）或向 Director 升级
- 易错点：OLMo 的 failure_reason 是 "process dead 5× with no sentinel/W&B"，说明进程跑了 ~1.7h 后才死，不是启动即崩——可能是生成阶段 OOM 或脚本逻辑错误而非语法错误。上一次修复只改了语法，root cause 可能不同
- GPT-2 XL 是第一个非 Pythia 模型的结果，如果 success 则跨模型泛化 claim 成立，对论文 story 至关重要

## 项目硬约束 (memory.md 节选)

## 约束

- Qwen2.5 系列代码占比过高，LoRA 无法覆盖，已验证不可用
- 根分区 ~30GB，模型/数据必须在 `/root/autodl-tmp/`
- OLMo 必须用 `olmo-1b-hf` 目录（不是 `olmo-1b`）
- DCOL margin_lr 不能超过 1e-4（1e-3 导致 embedding collapse）
- OLMo cron 监控有 false failure 风险（子进程 PID vs pipeline 主进程）

## 可查的历史
前 9 轮 handoff 在 `task_context/history/agent/loop_*.md`，如有疑问可 Read 回查。
完整聊天归档可用 `recall_session_history("关键词")` 搜索。

1. 用 `read_registry` 确认当前 RUNNING/PREPARING 实验状态（防止幻觉记忆过时状态）

    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

mean_surprisal (0.089 vs 0.088)\\n5. p75_ppl (0.069 vs 0.067)\\n\\n### Code contamination\\n| Depth | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|-------|----------|--------|---|\\n| d1 | 5.0% | 7.7% | +2.7pp |\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\n\\n### Analysis\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold. |\\\\n|-------|----------|--------|---|\\\\n| d1 | 5.0% | 7.7% | +2.7pp |\\\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\\\n\\\\n### Analysis\\\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold. confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. The filtered 3-class acc (72.52%) mitigates this.\\n6. **Conclusion: SUCCESS** — 4-class acc 76.25% >> 40% threshold, d1v2 AUC 0.8327 >> 0.6 threshold. SSH 到 gpu-6000pro-2，读取 `src/dcol_trainer.py` 全文\n2. Review 代码，重点检查：\n   a. margin_lr 默认值是否已改为 1e-4（不是 1e-3）\n   b.
